# R10 full actual right-gap handoff

**UNVERIFIED. Original target OPEN; all-depth upper closure not obtained.**

Allocation `94fa373cf827421a` used one native invocation and two substantive analytical approaches. Mathematical programs, numerical arrays, solvers, formal submissions, further agents and further model calls: zero. Parent owns the ledger; this invocation stops after delivery.

Read the complete `PROOF_ATTEMPT.md`. The same enclosing `m>=1536`, `epsilon=1/(4m)`, `beta=1-epsilon`, `c=(1+sqrt(2))(1+epsilon)`, `a=beta/c`, and `H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a*c^j,H_j)` are used throughout. Original target remains `lim W_n^*/U_n^ZJ=1`, with `W=2R`, or a certified full-function asymptotic separation with the original selected minimizer/radius and actual last iterate. The accepted `47/50` benchmark comparison still needs its full-class upper premise.

## First approach: actual leaf reserve and a paid right normal

For a child of mass s, with `D=d+2kappa s`, define

    r=P(w/D), q=P(y/d), nR=w-Dr,
    zR=[w-y+(D-s)q]/D, uR=r-zR,
    RR=Rem(w,D,zR), BR=Rem(w,D,q),
    ER=U_D(w)-U_d(y).

The actual convex weights give the exact row identity

    RR = D||uR||^2
       + (2/D) sum_right h_i <nR,r-g_i>
       + 2(D-s)/D <nR,r-q>.

Every row is nonnegative. Hence `<nR,r-q> <= D RR/[2(D-s)]`. This corrects the idea that the R9 normal has no available quantitative bound. It does not control `q-r` by RR; the R9 interval counterexample remains valid.

For the actual inherited physical leaf `h=1+a`, every compact convex body containing zero, every start, every `d>=1`, and every `16/17<=kappa<=1`, the new derivation proves

    E >= R,  E >= B/300,
    E >= eta R + (1-eta) B/300  for 0<=eta<=1.

One concrete all-body base is `(eta,gamma)=(3/4,1/1200)`. The proof keeps the two actual leaf projection rows, obtains an exact positive decomposition of `||x||^2-||y+hq||^2`, and handles `d>=2` and `1<=d<=2` with explicit inequalities. It is not a finite-body numerical check.

In the R9 parent, let `M=D+2kappa h`, `sigma=2kappa-1`, and suppose the right child satisfies `ER>=eta RR+gamma BR`. Then the right normal terms in a parent retention of the same eta are actually paid:

    M ER - 2 eta D<nR,uR> - 2 eta sigma h<nR,r-q>
      >= eta M D||uR||^2 + gamma M D||q-r||^2
       + (4 eta kappa h/D) sum_right h_i<nR,r-g_i>
       + (2 eta h d/D + 2 gamma M)<nR,r-q>.

The cancellation is `(M-D)(D-s)/D-sigma h=hd/D`. No independent normal value is introduced.

The remaining left normal is substantive. Using the exact identity

    nR=nL+(alpha-b)p+A u-D r,

the coefficient of `<nL,u>` after inserting child fraction eta and subtracting parent fraction eta' is

    2A[eta-1+(1-eta')theta].

Moreover

    A<nL,u> = sum_left h_i<nL,ell-g_i> + alpha<nL,ell-p>.

The boundary reserve BL separately controls only the terminal row in this fan. The direct termwise allocation therefore needs

    1-eta' >= (1-eta)/theta.

At kappa16/17, feasible theta is below3/4 for every j>=2. The concrete eta3/4 base consequently cannot propagate through arbitrary depth under this allocation: five such joins make the required loss exceed one. This is a failure of the stated allocation, not a proof that richer correlated-row payments or the two-remainder inequality itself are impossible.

The proof also retains the complete exact right gap:

    ER = D||r||^2-(D-2delta s)||q||^2-2D<uR,q>+2<nR,r-q>.

For theta0 and proposed full canonical retention, conditional on the actually unproved higher-child `ER>=RR`, its exact use yields the unpaid interface

    M(Psi-Rall) >= A(EL-RL)+J+h ER+h(D+h)||r||^2
                   -sigma h(D-2delta s+sigma h)||q||^2
                   -2delta A s||p||^2.

The normal cancels; the remaining actual terminal-norm payment was not proved. This expression is not offered as a completed certificate or another unproved PSD theorem.

## Second approach: scaled physical-displacement comparison actually fails

To reduce the left terminal weight, try

    z_lambda=[lambda(x-y)+(M-lambda S)q]/M,
    1<lambda<=2kappa.

It is feasible. The parent theta1 mixture has weights

    A ell + (lambda h-A)p + (M-lambda h)r,

so it avoids the old negative bridge coefficient when `lambda h>=A`. But the desired full retention `E>=Rem(x,M,z_lambda)` is false on actual leaves.

For every inherited leaf h=1+a, take

    kappa16/17, lambda3/2, d16,
    q=(1,0), g=(5/4,1), K=conv{0,q,g},
    T=16+(5/4)(h-1), x=g+T(1,-1/4), y=x-hg.

The projection normals prove `g=P(x)` and `q=P(y/16)` exactly. All comparison weights are positive. With `M=16+(32/17)h`,

    M[E-Rem(x,M,z_(3/2))]/h
      = -9M/68+(252-153h)/64 < -621/1088 <0.

The full U gap itself is positive on this same run:

    E=(16+5h/4)^2/(16+32h/17)-16 >0.

Therefore this is a counterexample to the fully retained repair, not to the U law. The same triangle construction with `g=(1+z,1)`, `z>(1-kappa)/(lambda-1)`, disproves the fixed-lambda fully retained law for every lambda>1 at sufficiently large trailing d. A d-dependent lambda, partial fraction or richer potential was not proved to compose.

## Exact remaining scope

There is no all-depth recurrence reaching final kappa at least16/17, no full W upper certificate on H_m, no original-target resolution and no complete candidate for formal verification. The packet supplies an actual all-body leaf base, an actual right-normal payment, the precise left-fan loss in the attempted recursion, and an admissible counterexample to the alternative fixed-lambda full-retention invariant.

No private worker memory was read. No service/worker, Goal, truth store, TARGET, CURRENT_RUN, OPERATOR, other packet, publication or budget was changed. File writes are only this proof and handoff. Hashes are reported to the parent after read-back; no resource allowance carries forward.

# R10: actual full-gap reserves, normal payment, and a failed displacement repair

**All new mathematics in this packet is UNVERIFIED under Danus policy. The all-depth upper bound is not established. The original target remains OPEN.**

Allocation `94fa373cf827421a`: one native analytical invocation, at most two substantive approaches. Exactly two approaches were used. Mathematical programs, numerical arrays, solvers, formal submissions, additional agents and additional model calls: zero. All derivations below were done analytically. The parent owns the resource ledger and shared administration.

The first approach obtains a genuine all-body leaf estimate retaining both the canonical maximization remainder and the endpoint comparison remainder. It also pays the two troublesome right-child normal terms in the R9 transport identity, using their actual convex weights. The selected two-remainder induction does not close: its remaining left initial-normal fan has an expanding loss of retained fraction under the direct termwise allocation. The second approach changes the comparison mixture by multiplying the physical displacement. A concrete actual two-dimensional leaf disproves the uniformly fully retained version needed by that repair. Neither failure is a counterexample to the desired U law.

## 1. Fixed target, parameters, and accepted inputs

The original problem is

\[
W_n(h)=\sup\frac{2(f(x_n)-f(x_\star))}{L\|x_0-x_\star\|^2},
\qquad W_n^*=\inf_{h\in[0,\infty)^n}W_n(h),
\qquad U_n^{\mathrm{ZJ}}=\frac1{B_n},
\]

and asks whether `lim W_n^*/U_n^ZJ = 1`, or for a certified full-function-class asymptotic separation. The supremum includes every finite dimension, every convex differentiable L-smooth function with an attained minimum, every originally selected minimizer and distinct start, and the actual final GD iterate. The normalization is `W = 2R`.

Fix ONE enclosing integer `m >= 1536` and use, at every child,

\[
\epsilon=\frac1{4m},\quad \beta=1-\epsilon,\quad
c=(1+\sqrt2)(1+\epsilon),\quad a=\frac\beta c,
\qquad H_0=(),\quad H_{j+1}=(H_j,1+ac^j,H_j).
\]

Write `S_j = sum H_j`. No parameter is reset at a child. The physical leaf is `H_1 = (h)` with `h = 1+a`; in this regime

\[
\frac43<h<\sqrt2<\frac32. \tag{1.1}
\]

Accepted `931b9653ce0f7201` gives

\[
\frac{B_{2^m-1}}{1+2S_m}<\frac{47}{50}.
\]

Thus a full-class bound `W(H_m) <= 17/[16(1+2S_m)]` for every sufficiently large m would give a strict `799/800` subsequence separation. Its upper-bound premise remains unproved.

Fix one arbitrary nonempty compact convex body `K` containing zero in a finite-dimensional Euclidean space. All physical and auxiliary points below use this one body. Set

\[
P=P_K,\qquad
F(x)=\max_{g\in K}\{\langle x,g\rangle-\tfrac12\|g\|^2\},
\qquad U_d(x)=\max_{g\in K}\{2\langle x,g\rangle-d\|g\|^2\}.
\]

Then `grad F = P`. For an actual sample `r = P(x/D)`, put `n = x-Dr`. Its normal property is

\[
\langle n,r-z\rangle\ge0\quad(z\in K).
\]

For any feasible `z in K`, define

`phi_D(x,z)=2<x,z>-D||z||^2` and

\[
\operatorname{Rem}(x,D,z)
=U_D(x)-2\langle x,z\rangle+D\|z\|^2
=D\|r-z\|^2+2\langle n,r-z\rangle. \tag{1.2}
\]

Accepted `1e0bf3f41457d8e4`, whose statement and complete Parts B/C were read, supplies the actual same-m H3 U law, the H1/H2 primitive certificates, and the finite-word supremum transfer. Higher primitive positivity is not assumed here.

For every selected minimizer `x_star in K^polar`, completion of the square gives

\[
U_D(x)\le\frac{\|x-x_\star\|^2}{D}. \tag{1.3}
\]

Consequently an all-body law

\[
U_1(T_{H_m}x)\le U_{1+(32/17)S_m}(x)
\]

would give the required full W bound, since

\[
\frac1{1+(32/17)S_m}\le\frac{17}{16(1+2S_m)}.
\]

This implication keeps the original selected minimizer and radius. No such all-depth premise is proved below.

## 2. Approach 1: retain the actual endpoint gap and pay the actual normals

### 2.1 A comparison remainder that contains the missing boundary motion

For an actual child of mass `s`, input `w` and endpoint `y`, take

\[
\frac{16}{17}\le\kappa\le1,\quad
\delta=1-\kappa,\quad\sigma=2\kappa-1,\quad
D=d+2\kappa s,\quad d\ge1,
\]

\[
r=P(w/D),\qquad q=P(y/d),\qquad n_R=w-Dr.
\]

The two distinct remainders are

\[
z_R=\frac{w-y+(D-s)q}{D},\quad
u_R=r-z_R,\quad R_R=\operatorname{Rem}(w,D,z_R),
\]

\[
\mathcal B_R=\operatorname{Rem}(w,D,q)
=D\|r-q\|^2+2\langle n_R,r-q\rangle. \tag{2.1}
\]

The actual gap is `E_R = U_D(w)-U_d(y)`. Unlike `R_R`, the remainder `B_R` directly contains the missing endpoint difference. This definition assigns no independent values to the two remainders.

Writing `w-y = sum_i h_i g_i`, define the actual nonnegative rows

\[
N_{R,i}=\langle n_R,r-g_i\rangle,
\qquad N_{R,q}=\langle n_R,r-q\rangle.
\]

The convex weights in `z_R` give the exact identity

\[
R_R=D\|u_R\|^2
 +\frac2D\sum_i h_iN_{R,i}
 +\frac{2(D-s)}D N_{R,q}. \tag{2.2}
\]

All coefficients are nonnegative, and `D-s > 0`. In particular,

\[
N_{R,q}\le\frac{D}{2(D-s)}R_R. \tag{2.3}
\]

This is a proved bound on the real normal exposed in R9. The vector `q-r` still need not be controlled by `R_R`: the R9 interval example has `R_R=0` and `q-r != 0`. Equation (2.3) does not make that invalid vector estimate.

### 2.2 A genuine all-body leaf base

For the actual inherited leaf `h=1+a`, let

\[
g=P(x),\quad y=x-hg,\quad q=P(y/d),\quad
D=d+2\kappa h,\quad r=P(x/D),\quad n=x-Dr,
\]

\[
z=q+\frac hD(g-q),\quad u=r-z,\quad
R=\operatorname{Rem}(x,D,z),\quad
\mathcal B=\operatorname{Rem}(x,D,q),\quad
E=U_D(x)-U_d(y).
\]

**Leaf claim.** For every body, start, `d >= 1`, and `16/17 <= kappa <= 1` just specified,

\[
E\ge R,\qquad E\ge\frac{\mathcal B}{300}.
\tag{2.4}
\]

Consequently, for every `eta in [0,1]`,

\[
E\ge\eta R+\frac{1-\eta}{300}\mathcal B.
\tag{2.5}
\]

One explicit base is `eta = 3/4`, with boundary coefficient `1/1200`. These are proved leaf constants, not suggested values for higher children.

Here is a complete proof. Put `t=g-q`, and retain the two actual rows

\[
N_g=\langle x-g,g-q\rangle\ge0,
\qquad N_q=\langle y-dq,q-g\rangle\ge0.
\]

Define

\[
V_d=\|x\|^2-\|y+hq\|^2.
\]

Since `y+hq=x-ht`, expansion yields `V_d=2h<x,t>-h^2||t||^2`. Also

\[
N_g=\langle x,t\rangle-\langle g,t\rangle,
\quad N_q=-\langle x,t\rangle+h\langle g,t\rangle+d\langle q,t\rangle.
\]

Therefore

\[
V_d=A_gN_g+A_qN_q+\zeta\|t\|^2, \tag{2.6}
\]

where

\[
A_g=\frac{2h(h+d)}{h+d-1},\quad
A_q=\frac{2h}{h+d-1},\quad
\zeta=h\left(\frac{2d}{h+d-1}-h\right).
\]

The denominators are positive. For `h>1`, the fraction `2d/(h+d-1)` increases with d, so `zeta >= 2-h^2 > 0` by (1.1). In particular `V_d >= 0`.

The discounted exact gap identity gives

\[
E=\frac{V_d}{D}+2\delta h\|q\|^2+R. \tag{2.7}
\]

Thus `E >= R`. It remains to control the boundary vector.

Let `N_rq=<n,r-q>` and `N_rg=<n,r-g>`. Both are nonnegative. By the two convex weights of z,

\[
R=D\|u\|^2+\frac{2h}{D}N_{rg}
                   +\frac{2(D-h)}D N_{rq}. \tag{2.8}
\]

Here `D>2h`: indeed `D-2h >= 1-(2/17)h > 0`. Because `r-q=u+(h/D)t`,

\[
\mathcal B
\le 2D\|u\|^2+\frac{2h^2}{D}\|t\|^2+2N_{rq}
\le4R+2T,
\quad T:=\frac{h^2}{D}\|t\|^2. \tag{2.9}
\]

For `d >= 2`, (1.1) gives

\[
\frac\zeta h\ge\frac4{h+1}-h\ge\frac1{10}.
\]

Consequently `T <= 15 V_d/D <= 15E`, and (2.9) gives `B <= 34E`.

Now suppose `1 <= d <= 2`. Set

\[
\tau=\frac{D-1}{\sigma h},\qquad
\tau-1=\frac{d+h-1}{\sigma h}.
\]

The exact normal identities

\[
x-g=(h-1)t+(D-1)q+Du+n,
\qquad y-dq=\sigma hq+Du+n
\]

give

\[
(h-1)\|t\|^2
=(\tau-1)\bigl(D\langle u,t\rangle+\langle n,t\rangle\bigr)
+N_g+\tau N_q. \tag{2.10}
\]

Since `<n,t>=N_rq-N_rg <= N_rq`, Young's inequality gives

\[
\begin{aligned}
\|t\|^2\le{}&
\frac{(\tau-1)^2D^2}{(h-1)^2}\|u\|^2
+\frac{2(\tau-1)}{h-1}N_{rq}\\
&+\frac2{h-1}N_g+\frac{2\tau}{h-1}N_q.
\end{aligned} \tag{2.11}
\]

For this range, the elementary bounds

\[
h\le\frac32,\quad h-1\ge\frac13,\quad
\sigma\ge\frac{15}{17},\quad D-h>2,
\quad \tau-1\le\frac{17}{8},\quad \tau\le\frac{25}{8}
\]

are sufficient. Multiply (2.11) by `h^2/D`. Using (2.6)--(2.8), its four terms are bounded by, respectively,

\[
\frac{23409}{256}E\le92E,\qquad
\frac{459}{64}E\le8E,\qquad
\frac92E\le5E,\qquad
\frac{1125}{32}E\le36E.
\]

For the third bound use `A_g >= 2h`; for the fourth use `A_q=2h/(h+d-1)`. For the second use `N_rq <= D E/[2(D-h)]`. Thus `T <= 141E`, and (2.9) gives `B <= 286E <= 300E`. This proves (2.4). Taking the convex combination of its two lower bounds proves (2.5).

The proof used actual projection rows, including the initial and endpoint auxiliary samples. It did not free the vectors or normals. The deliberately conservative coefficient `1/300` is compatible with the much smaller-than-one boundary fraction forced by the core-quadratic leaf calibration.

### 2.3 Exact payment of the right normals in the parent transport

Use the R9 actual parent notation

\[
s=S_j,\quad b=1+k,\quad h=s+b,\quad C=1+s,
\quad S=s+h,
\]

\[
A=1+2\kappa s,\quad\alpha=A-s,\quad
D=d+2\kappa s,\quad M=D+2\kappa h.
\]

The physical parent is `x -> v`, `p=P(v)`, `w=v-bp`, then `w -> y`. Put

\[
\ell=P(x/A),\quad
z_L=\frac{x-v+\alpha p}{A},\quad u=\ell-z_L,
\quad n_L=x-A\ell,
\]

and retain the right variables in (2.1). Set `R_L=Rem(x,A,z_L)` and

\[
\mathcal B_L=\operatorname{Rem}(x,A,p),
\quad E_L=U_A(x)-U_1(v),\quad
\Psi=U_M(x)-U_d(y).
\]

For a feasible `theta`, R9 uses

\[
0\le\theta\le\min(1,b/\alpha),\qquad
z_\theta=z_P+\frac{\theta A}{M}u,
\]

where `z_P=[x-w+(M-h)r]/M`. Define

\[
z_{\rm all}=\frac{x-y+(M-S)q}{M},\quad
R_{\rm all}=\operatorname{Rem}(x,M,z_{\rm all}),\quad
R_\theta=\operatorname{Rem}(x,M,z_\theta).
\]

Its complete canonical transport is

\[
t_0=-Du_R+\sigma h(q-r),\qquad
t=t_0-\theta Au,
\qquad n_\theta=n_R-\sigma hr-\theta Au,
\]

\[
M(R_{\rm all}-R_\theta)=-2\langle n_\theta,t\rangle+\|t\|^2. \tag{2.12}
\]

Consider the two-remainder induction statement actually selected for this approach:

The coefficients satisfy `0 <= eta_j <= 1` and `gamma_j >= 0`.

\[
E_j\ge\eta_jR_j+\gamma_j\mathcal B_j
\quad\text{for every body, start, and trailing }d\ge1.
\tag{2.13}
\]

The leaf base (2.5) establishes `(eta_1,gamma_1)=(3/4,1/1200)`. The right normals in the transport can be paid without losing a common retained fraction. More precisely, suppose the right child satisfies (2.13) with `eta,gamma`, and the proposed parent canonical fraction is also eta. The relevant right-normal expression is

\[
\mathcal R_N
=M E_R-2\eta D\langle n_R,u_R\rangle
             -2\eta\sigma hN_{R,q}.
\]

Using (2.2), the child statement gives the explicit lower bound

\[
\begin{aligned}
\mathcal R_N\ge{}&
\eta MD\|u_R\|^2+\gamma MD\|q-r\|^2\\
&+\frac{4\eta\kappa h}{D}\sum_i h_iN_{R,i}
+\left(\frac{2\eta hd}{D}+2\gamma M\right)N_{R,q}.
\end{aligned} \tag{2.14}
\]

Every displayed coefficient is nonnegative. The terminal coefficient follows from the exact cancellation

\[
(M-D)\frac{D-s}{D}-\sigma h
=2\kappa h\frac{D-s}{D}-\sigma h
=\frac{hd}{D}. \tag{2.15}
\]

This is an actual payment of both right normal terms, not an assumed normal bound and not a PSD condition. It uses the real right-child gradients with their original step weights.

### 2.4 Attempted preservation and the remaining left fan

The full R9 identity, with all signs retained, is

\[
M\Psi=A E_L+M E_R+J+M R_\theta+T_\theta,
\]

\[
\begin{aligned}
T_\theta={}&-(1-\theta)A R_L
+\theta(1-\theta)A^2\|u\|^2\\
&+2\theta A\langle(\alpha-b)p-(M-h)r,u\rangle
+2\delta(Mh\|r\|^2-As\|p\|^2).
\end{aligned} \tag{2.16}
\]

Here

\[
\begin{aligned}
J={}&\Theta\|p-r\|^2
+2h\left(1+\frac C{D+k}\right)N_p
+\frac{2hC}{D+k}N_r,\\
\Theta={}&\frac{h[CD-k(C+D+k)]}{D+k},\\
N_p={}&\langle v-p,p-r\rangle,\qquad
N_r=\langle n_R,r-p\rangle.
\end{aligned}
\]

These are actual rows and both discount squares in (2.16) are indispensable.

Subtracting a proposed parent fraction `eta' R_all` introduces the term

\[
-2\eta'\theta A\langle n_R,u\rangle.
\]

The exact relation

\[
n_R=n_L+(\alpha-b)p+Au-Dr \tag{2.17}
\]

shows which left normal must be paid. After inserting a left-child bound with canonical fraction eta, the coefficient of `<n_L,u>` is exactly

\[
2A[\eta-1+(1-\eta')\theta]. \tag{2.18}
\]

This can also be written on its individual actual projection rows. Define

\[
N_{L,i}=\langle n_L,\ell-g_i\rangle\ge0,
\qquad N_{L,p}=\langle n_L,\ell-p\rangle\ge0,
\]

with i ranging over the actual nonterminal left queries. Then

\[
A\langle n_L,u\rangle
=\sum_i h_iN_{L,i}+\alpha N_{L,p}. \tag{2.19}
\]

The normal part of `gamma B_L` controls `N_L,p`, but has no separate positive coefficient on the first sum in (2.19). The root rows in J are at p and r, and (2.14) pays right rows. Under the direct termwise allocation used here, avoiding a negative coefficient on this left fan therefore requires

\[
\eta\ge1-(1-\eta')\theta,
\quad\text{or, when }\theta>0,\quad
1-\eta'\ge\frac{1-\eta}{\theta}. \tag{2.20}
\]

In this allocation the remaining `R_theta` is used only as a nonnegative whole; no comparison of its different maximizing normal with `n_L` has been established. This is a failure of this allocation, not a necessary condition for every possible all-body proof: correlations with additional original rows could still pay the fan. They have not been proved to do so here.

The loss in (2.20) is material. At the intended fixed `kappa=16/17`, the R9 scalar bounds `k/C<5/12` and `C>5` give

\[
\theta\le\frac b\alpha
<\frac{17(12+5C)}{12(15C+2)}<\frac34
\qquad(j\ge2). \tag{2.21}
\]

The last inequality follows from `C>5>93/25`. Thus this termwise scheme expands an existing loss `1-eta` by a factor greater than `4/3` at each such join. Starting with the explicit leaf choice `eta=3/4`, the loss exceeds one after five joins having this bound. It cannot propagate to arbitrary depth. Taking theta zero demands eta one already at the child.

The full boundary reserve solved the right-normal debt, but did not solve this left fan. No claim is made that (2.13) itself is false, or that a negative independent-vector matrix represents an actual counterexample. The attempted recurrence has failed before a valid final kappa sequence can be produced.

### 2.5 A direct use of the whole right gap, beyond its two lower bounds

I also kept the exact dependence of `E_R` on the terminal auxiliary projection rather than replacing the whole gap by (2.13). Eliminating the physical displacement through `z_R` gives

\[
E_R=D\|r\|^2-(D-2\delta s)\|q\|^2
-2D\langle u_R,q\rangle+2N_{R,q}. \tag{2.22}
\]

This identity is useful because the `u_R` cross term is real child information. For example, conditionally assuming `E_R >= R_R`, take `theta=0` and proposed parent fraction one. The exact transport, (2.22), and the bound

\[
2D\langle n_R,u_R\rangle\le D E_R-D^2\|u_R\|^2
\]

give

\[
\begin{aligned}
M(\Psi-R_{\rm all})\ge{}&
A(E_L-R_L)+J+hE_R
+h(D+h)\|r\|^2\\
&-\sigma h(D-2\delta s+\sigma h)\|q\|^2
-2\delta As\|p\|^2.
\end{aligned} \tag{2.23}
\]

In obtaining (2.23), the `N_R,q` terms cancel exactly and
`sigma^2+4 delta kappa=1` is used. Thus even this stronger treatment genuinely pays the troublesome normal. It leaves a quantitative relation among the actual right terminal norm, the actual complete right gap and the root/left reserves. Neither `E_R >= 0` nor the small leaf boundary fraction proves that relation. Equation (2.23) is recorded as the exact unpaid interface of this attempt, not as an upper certificate or as a new theorem-level reduction claimed to solve the task. I did not assume `E_R >= R_R` at unproved higher children.

## 3. Approach 2: scaled displacement and an actual counterexample to full retention

### 3.1 The proposed repair

A possible way to remove the negative bridge coefficient at theta one is to use a different feasible comparison point. For a physical word of mass S, define

\[
z_\lambda=\frac{\lambda(x-y)+(M-\lambda S)q}{M},
\qquad M=d+2\kappa S,
\quad q=P(y/d),
\quad 1<\lambda\le2\kappa. \tag{3.1}
\]

It is a convex combination of the same physical gradients and q. For a left child the endpoint weight is `A-lambda s`. In a parent prefix, replacing its left comparison by the actual maximizer at theta one gives the weights

\[
A\ell+(\lambda h-A)p+(M-\lambda h)r. \tag{3.2}
\]

Thus theta one is feasible whenever `lambda h >= A`, in addition to the other nonnegative weights. The physical displacement has not been changed: lambda only changes a comparison mixture.

The fully retained induction one would like for this repair is

\[
U_M(x)-U_d(y)\ge\operatorname{Rem}(x,M,z_\lambda). \tag{3.3}
\]

For `lambda=1` this is the canonical fully retained law. A fixed larger lambda would provide the changed endpoint weight uniformly at every trailing parameter. However, (3.3) is false on actual leaves. This is not merely a failed choice of dual multipliers.

### 3.2 Exact bare-gap formula for one actual leaf

Take one physical leaf h, put `g=P(x)`, `y=x-hg`, `q=P(y/d)`, and `t=g-q`. Write `M=d+2 kappa h`. Multiplying the difference between the two sides of (3.3) by M gives

\[
\begin{aligned}
\mathcal D_\lambda
:={}&M\left[U_M(x)-U_d(y)-\operatorname{Rem}(x,M,z_\lambda)\right]\\
={}&2\delta Mh\|q\|^2
+2\lambda h\langle x,t\rangle
-2hM(\lambda-1)\langle q,t\rangle
-\lambda^2h^2\|t\|^2.
\end{aligned} \tag{3.4}
\]

This follows by writing `M z_lambda = Mq+lambda h t` and `y=x-h(q+t)` in `M[phi_M(x,z_lambda)-U_d(y)]`. In particular, if the actual initial projection row satisfies `<x-g,g-q>=0`, then

\[
\mathcal D_\lambda
=2\delta Mh\|q\|^2
+2h[\lambda-M(\lambda-1)]\langle q,t\rangle
+\lambda h(2-\lambda h)\|t\|^2. \tag{3.5}
\]

The following construction realizes this equality with one fixed compact body and the actual GD update.

### 3.3 An explicit inherited H1 counterexample

Keep the actual enclosing m and its leaf `h=1+a`. Set

\[
\kappa=\frac{16}{17},\quad \lambda=\frac32,\quad d=16,
\qquad q=(1,0),\quad g=(5/4,1),
\qquad K=\operatorname{conv}\{0,q,g\}.
\]

This is a compact full-dimensional triangle containing zero. Put

\[
T=16+\frac54(h-1),\qquad
n_g=T(1,-1/4),\qquad x=g+n_g.
\]

The initial projection is exactly g. Indeed

\[
\langle n_g,q-g\rangle=0,
\qquad \langle n_g,-g\rangle=-T<0.
\]

The actual leaf endpoint is

\[
y=x-hg
=\left(16,-(h-1)-\frac T4\right).
\]

Its auxiliary projection at the required trailing parameter is exactly q: the vector

\[
y-16q=\left(0,-(h-1)-\frac T4\right)
\]

has nonpositive inner product with both `g-q=(1/4,1)` and `0-q=(-1,0)`. Thus `q=P(y/16)`. No projection value was assigned freely.

Here `t=(1/4,1)`, so `<q,t>=1/4`, `||t||^2=17/16`, and `<x,t>=<g,t>`. Formula (3.5), with `M=16+(32/17)h`, becomes exactly

\[
\frac{\mathcal D_{3/2}}h
=-\frac9{68}M+\frac{252-153h}{64}. \tag{3.6}
\]

Because `h>1` and `M>16`,

\[
\frac{\mathcal D_{3/2}}h
< -\frac{36}{17}+\frac{99}{64}
=-\frac{621}{1088}<0. \tag{3.7}
\]

All mixture weights in (3.1) are positive since `lambda=3/2 < 32/17=2kappa`. Consequently the comparison point is feasible, but (3.3) nevertheless fails strictly. This holds for every allowed enclosing m and its actual physical H1 leaf.

The full U gap is positive on this very example. In fact

\[
x_1=16+\frac54h,\qquad x_2=1-\frac T4<0,
\quad r=P(x/M)=\left(\frac{x_1}{M},0\right),
\]

because `0<x_1/M<1`. The lower edge of the triangle supplies the downward normal at r. Therefore

\[
E=U_M(x)-U_{16}(y)
=\frac{(16+5h/4)^2}{16+32h/17}-16>0, \tag{3.8}
\]

whose numerator after bringing to a common denominator is

\[
\frac{168}{17}h+\frac{25}{16}h^2>0.
\]

Thus the counterexample rejects only the proposed full-retention repair. It does not reject the U upper bound, even for this leaf.

### 3.4 Why choosing another fixed lambda greater than one does not repair this family

The same construction has a useful quantified extension. Fix any `lambda>1`, `kappa<=1`, and physical leaf `h>1`, with `lambda<=2kappa` to keep (3.1) feasible. Choose any fixed

\[
z>\frac{1-\kappa}{\lambda-1},\quad
q=(1,0),\quad g=(1+z,1),\quad
K=\operatorname{conv}\{0,q,g\}.
\]

For a trailing d set

\[
T=d+(h-1)(1+z),\quad x=g+T(1,-z),\quad y=x-hg.
\]

The same two normal checks show `g=P(x)` and `q=P(y/d)` for every `d>=1`. The body and physical leaf are fixed before the run; changing d only selects another allowed start and auxiliary query. Equation (3.5) becomes

\[
\frac{\mathcal D_\lambda}{h}
=2[\delta-(\lambda-1)z]M
+2\lambda z+\lambda(2-\lambda h)(z^2+1). \tag{3.9}
\]

Its coefficient of `M=d+2kappa h` is strictly negative. Hence (3.3) fails for every sufficiently large d. For example any d making

\[
M>\frac{\left|2\lambda z+\lambda(2-\lambda h)(z^2+1)\right|+1}
          {2[(\lambda-1)z-\delta]}
\]

suffices. This rejects the whole fixed-lambda, fully retained, all-trailing-parameter variant of this repair. A lambda that depends on d, a smaller retained fraction, or additional terms would be a different invariant; none was proved to compose in this allocation.

## 4. What remains unpaid and what this packet does not conclude

The normal exposed by R9 is no longer an unexplained independent obstruction: (2.2)--(2.3) establish its actual weight, and (2.14)--(2.15) pay both right normal terms jointly without spending an extra canonical fraction. The leaf estimate (2.5) is a quantified all-body base carrying endpoint motion and the normal together.

The direct two-remainder preservation still lacks a payment for the left initial-normal fan (2.19) and the remaining coupled vector terms. Its simple termwise bookkeeping forces (2.20), which loses the concrete leaf reserve too quickly. Keeping the complete exact right gap instead yields (2.23), whose terminal-norm payment was not established from actual children. These are precise failures of the two attempted preservation calculations, not statements that every richer Lyapunov function must fail.

The alternative comparison mixture makes theta one feasible algebraically, but the full-retention property required by the fixed-lambda version is actually false, by (3.3)--(3.9). Feasibility of a comparison point does not establish a lower bound on the gap remaining after its maximization remainder has been subtracted.

There is no all-depth coefficient recurrence with final `kappa_m >= 16/17`, no full-class upper certificate for H_m, no asymptotic counterexample to the original conjecture, and no all-schedule lower proof in this packet. The original Goal remains OPEN. No complete target-level candidate is available for an independent adversarial audit or formal submission.

Neither source licenses applying primitive or s-composable composition to the unproved higher H_j children. # Full local seven-step geometry: base rigidity and the remaining cone

Status: COMPLETE JOINT ANALYTICAL DRAFT, UNVERIFIED. Worker max2, ONE round
after completed46, under TASK.md and master_guidance f8408daf213a461d;
plan d1e013ea01c97469. No fact submission is authorized or made. Neither

The new calculation covers EVERY base certificate: actual equality
witnesses confine its remainder to one ray; an original multiplier sign
eliminates the ray; the entire base array is then unique. Uniqueness is
derived, not assumed. Six necessary physical tangent inequalities follow.
They leave a strict positive-capacity direction. The full original
sign/PSD linearization, an attempted completion, and the exact quadratic
correction are given below. Their unresolved feasibility is distinguished
from the contact test.

primitive identity and composition; 1252b8823bb5a290 for interpolation
orientation; 5e1848ee0b153f5f for the conditional length-seven interface
A>lambda^3. Earlier drafts, including 8ed2e5206744ba63 and ff8b941536cb035e,
are awareness only. Every additional result used below is proved jointly
in this UNVERIFIED manuscript.

## 1. Original objects and a supplied base array

Put q=sqrt(2), lambda=1+q, u=q-1=1/lambda, and

 hbar=(q,2,q,1+lambda,q,2,q), Abar=lambda^3.

For a nearby nonnegative word h of length7 set s=sum h_i, A=1+s. Retain
all eight independent formal gradients. For a zero-diagonal array Lambda,

 e=(1,...,1)^T in R8,
 T(h)_ii=1, T(h)_ij=h_j for j<i, T(h)_ij=0 for j>i,
 L(Lambda)=diag(Lambda e)-Lambda,
 omega(h)=(-2h_0,...,-2h_6,2s)^T,
 D(h)=diag(h_0(2-h_0),...,h_6(2-h_6),-s(s+2)),
 M(h,Lambda)=D(h)+sym(L(Lambda)T(h)), sym(X)=(X+X^T)/2.       (1)

Also skew(X)=(X-X^T)/2 throughout.

The original conditions are ALL56 off-diagonal signs Lambda_ij>=0,
L^T e=omega, and M>=0 on the ENTIRE R8. On the original positions
x_i=x_0-sum_(k<i)h_k g_k, put

 Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2,
 V_h=2sum_(i<7)h_i(f_i-f_7)
       +sum_(i<7)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2-s(s+1)||g_7||^2.

Accepted04219554e41d9750 gives the formal identity

 V_h+sum Lambda_ij Q_ij=<M,Gram(g_0,...,g_7)>.                (2)

The identity requires flow, not legality of the jets. Actual smooth
convex jets have Q_ij<=0.

One complete base array Lambdabar has these TWENTY positive entries.

 ij | Lambdabar_ij

 21 | 2lambda q

Its LT has diagonal(-2u,2lambda) and off-diagonal(-2,2); D has diagonal
(2u,-2lambda), so M=0. Joining two singletons uses bridge2, capacity
lambda^2, right scaling kappa=lambda^2 and extra factor kappa-1=2lambda.
The accepted join identity gives the first seven entries in the table
on local indices0,...,3. Joining two of these children uses bridge

37:2lambda^2,43:2lambda q,53:4lambda,63:2lambda q,73:2lambda, and adds

## 2. Actual static equality witnesses

For a compact convex K containing0 define

 F_K(x)=max_(g in K){<x,g>-||g||^2/2}.                        (3)

Completing the square gives grad F_K(x)=P_K(x). A proposed g is the
projection iff g in K and <x-g,z-g><=0 for every z in K. Adding the
normal inequalities at two projections proves firm nonexpansiveness
and hence 1-Lipschitzness. The two maximizing inequalities in(3) bound
the error after its linear term by ||z||^2 under displacement z, proving
differentiability. A supremum of affine functions is convex. If0 is
interior to K, a small positive multiple of each nonzero x in K gives
F_K(x)>0, so the unique minimizer is0.

At actual queries, put y_i=x_i-g_i and
f_i=<x_i,g_i>-||g_i||^2/2. Direct expansion gives EVERY directed row,
including both optimum orientations,

 Q_ij=<y_j,g_i-g_j>.                                        (4)

A normal check against all vertices therefore checks all56 finite rows
and all optimum rows. The GD norm telescope gives

 V_h=||x_0||^2-||x_7+s g_7||^2.                             (5)

Equation(5) also holds for formal data satisfying GD and the displayed
value formula, regardless of whether their gradients are projections.

(a) A scalar Huber function started sufficiently far into its positive
linear part supplies the gradient column e and V=Q=0. The unit quadratic
started at1 supplies

 vbar=(1,-u,u,-u^2,u,-u^2,u^2,-u^3)^T,                       (6)

also V=Q=0. For any h, v=T(h)^(-1)e obeys
v_0=1,v_(i+1)=(1-h_i)v_i, and this quadratic has energy

 chi(h)=1-A^2 v_7^2.                                       (7)

This follows by telescoping h_i(2-h_i)v_i^2=v_i^2-v_(i+1)^2.

(b) For K=[-u,1], start at lambda^2. All prefix gradients through3
are1; x3=1; x4=-lambda. Every right gradient is -u and x7=-u.
These are projections onto one fixed interval, with gradient column

 cbar=(1,1,1,1,-u,-u,-u,-u)^T.                              (8)

The true initial distance is lambda^2, the unique minimum is0, and
V=lambda^4-lambda^6 u^2=0.

(c) Root quadrilateral. Fix theta in(-1,1), let ||U||=||V||=1 and
U dot V=theta, and put G=-(lambda U+V)/q,
K=conv{U,G,V,-V}. Start at lambda^2 U. Actual gradients are

 U,U,U,U,G,V,-V,uV.                                        (9)

Prefix positions through3 are the remaining prefix sums plus1, times U;
x4=-lambda U,x5=V,x6=-V,x7=uV. Every GD equality holds. Only the prefix
normals at0,1,2 and y4=(-U+V)/q can be nonzero. Prefix support follows
from U dot G=-(lambda+theta)/q<1 and |theta|<1. At4,

 y4 dot(V-G)=0,
 y4 dot(U-G)=2(theta-1)/q<0,
 y4 dot(-V-G)=-2(1-theta)/q<0.

All other normals vanish. The positive relation lambda U+qG+V=0 puts0
inside a triangle in K. Formula(4) checks every row, the true radius is
lambda^2, and(5) gives V=0. Its two coordinate columns span

 aR=(1,1,1,1,-lambda/q,0,0,0)^T,
 bR=(0,0,0,0,-1/q,1,-1,u)^T.                               (10)

(d) Left quadrilateral WITH the original right block. Fix theta in(-1,1),
let ||U||=1,||V||=u,U dot V=u theta, and put
G=-(U+V)/q,K=conv{U,G,V,-lambda V}. Start at lambda U. The data are

 g=(U,U,G,V,-lambda V,V,-V,uV),
 x1=U,x2=-U,x3=V,x4=-lambda V,x5=V,x6=-V,x7=uV.              (11)

Only y0=qU and y2=(-uU+V)/q can be nonzero. Write k=U dot V.
The prefix normal supports all vertices since -u<k<u,
U dot G=-(1+k)/q<1 and -lambda k<1. At2,

 y2 dot(V-G)=0,
 y2 dot(U-G)=k-u<0,
 y2 dot(-lambda V-G)=-(lambda+1)(u^2-u k)/q<0.

The first equality uses u^2+2u=1. The remaining queries have zero normal
and belong to K, including both occurrences of V. The relation U+qG+V=0
puts0 in its interior. All rows follow from(4), the true radius is lambda,
and V=lambda^2-lambda^6 u^4=0. Coordinate columns span

 aL=(1,1,-1/q,0,0,0,0,0)^T,
 bL=(0,0,-1/q,1,-lambda,1,-1,u)^T.                          (12)

Repeated ACTUAL gradients in these witnesses identify no formal
certificate coordinates.

## 3. A single possible remainder ray for EVERY base array

On any equality witness, identity(2) for any feasible array has a
nonpositive left side and a nonnegative right side. Both are zero.
Every strict residual forces its multiplier to vanish. Moreover M
annihilates every coordinate column of the gradient matrix. Indeed a
PSD quadratic with zero value annihilates the vector, as follows by
testing that vector plus t*w for arbitrary real t and w.

Thus M annihilates e,vbar,cbar,aR,bR,aL,bL. Here is the full rank check.
For z orthogonal to those columns, aL and bR give
z0+z1=z2/q and z5-z6+u z7=z4/q. Then bL gives
z3=z2/q+lambda*z4/q; substituting in aR gives lambda*z2=0.
Consequently z2=0,z0+z1=0,z3=lambda*z4/q. The e and cbar equations
force z3=z4=0 and z5+z6+z7=0. Together with bR they give
z5=-z7/q,z6=-u*z7/q. Finally vbar gives q*z0=0.
The common orthogonal complement is exactly the line

 z47=(0,0,0,0,0,-1/q,-u/q,1)^T.

Therefore EVERY feasible base remainder has

 M=tau*z47*z47^T, tau>=0.                                  (13)

Strict rows of the quadrilaterals also force

 Lambda_(i,j)=0 for i=4,5,6,7 and j=0,1,2;
 Lambda_(i,4)=0 for i=0,1,2,3,6,7;
 Lambda_(i,0)=0 for i=2,...,7;
 Lambda_(i,2)=0 for i=0,1,4,6,7.                            (14)

These are consequences, not a chosen support. Unlisted signs remain.

## 4. The original Lambda56 sign eliminates that ray; the base is unique

We reproduce the saturated-cut algebra and retain its possible signed
terminal coefficients. Suppose left/right capacities are a,b, bridge
1+d, d(a+b+d)=ab, C=a+b+d, kappa=(C/b)^2. Let P be left gradient
indices ending at bridge query p, B right indices ending at z.
The clipped equality trajectory has prefix gradient1, right gradient
-eta, eta=d/b=a/C. If R_i^a,R_j^b are the remaining child step sums, (4)
gives its cross residuals

 Q_ij=-(1+eta)eta R_j^b, Q_ji=-(1+eta)R_i^a
                       (i in P,j in B).                   (15)

Positive steps and complementarity leave only cross edges i->z and j->p.
Write their coefficients nu_i,mu_j. The common and clipped gradient
columns span the block indicators, so M kills both. Directly from(1),

 (M e_B)_i=-b*nu_i/2                         (i<p),
 (M e_B)_p=(-b*nu_p+d*sum mu_j)/2,
 (M e_P)_j=(d*mu_j-nu_p*h_j)/2               (p<j<z),
 (M e_P)_z=(d*mu_z-nu_p)/2.                                (16)

To check the first two formulas: T e_B is0 on P and the local right
prefix-capacity vector on B, ending at b. L^T e_B equals -sum mu at p
and omega_j+sum nu*1_(j=z) on B. The block flow is
sum mu-sum nu=2(a+d). Substitution gives the first two rows. For the
last two, T e_P equals a+d on B and a at p; L T e_P has entries d mu_j
on B, and T^T L^T e_P has entries -nu_p h_j and -nu_p at z.

Equations(16) and block flow force

 nu_i=0 (i<p), nu_p=d(kappa-1),
 mu_j=(kappa-1)h_j (j<z), mu_z=kappa-1.                     (17)

In fact sum mu=b*nu_p/d, so nu_p(b/d-1)=2(a+d); dC=ab gives(17).
Subtract these cross terms using the accepted join identity as a formal
identity. The remainder is block diagonal:

 M=M_left+kappa*M_right,
 Lambda^right_ij=Lambda_ij/kappa
                 -(1-1/kappa)h_i*1_(i<z,j=z).             (18)

Both blocks have their own exact flow and are PSD. Left coefficients are
ordinary. Right coefficients can be signed ONLY into z; their other
signs remain nonnegative. This qualification cannot be dropped.

Apply(15)--(18) at the central cut of hbar:
a=b=lambda^2,d=lambda,kappa=lambda^2. All five root cross entries in
Section1 are forced. Equation(13) gives M_left=0 and
M_right=(tau/kappa)*zeta*zeta^T, where zeta=(0,-1/q,-u/q,1)^T on local
right indices0,...,3. Subtract the basic three-step array from that right
array, calling the difference Delta and t=tau/kappa. Its flow is zero.
By(14), Delta20=Delta30=0.

Name the remaining entries

 Delta10=a0, Delta01=b0, Delta02=c0, Delta03=d0,
 Delta12=e0, Delta13=f0, Delta21=g0, Delta23=k0,
 Delta31=l0, Delta32=m0.

For local word(q,2,q), the matrix L(Delta)T is explicitly

 [-u(b0+c0+d0), -b0-2c0-2d0, -c0-q*d0, -d0],
 [u*a0,          a0-e0-f0,   -e0-q*f0, -f0],
 [0,             g0,         g0-u*k0, -k0],
 [0,             l0,         q*l0+u*m0,l0+m0].              (19)

Its symmetric part is t*zeta*zeta^T. Zero row/column0 force
d0=c0=b0=a0=0. Flow, diagonal entries, and entry13 give

 e0+f0=-t/2, l0=f0-q*t,
 g0=(q-1/2)t-f0, m0=lambda*t-f0, k0=t-f0,
 g0-u*k0=t*u^2/2.

The last equality gives f0=t/q. Hence all remaining entries are

 e0=-lambda*t/2, f0=t/q, g0=u*t/2,
 k0=u*t/q, l0=-t/q, m0=lambda*t/q.                         (20)

The other mixed entries check explicitly:
(-e0-q*f0+g0)/2=t*u/2 and
(-k0+q*l0+u*m0)/2=-t*u/q. Thus no equation is missing.
The child's entry12 has basic value0 and equals -lambda*t/2.
It targets a NONTERMINAL index, so(18) requires its nonnegativity.
This forces t=tau=0. Equivalently the original parent entry
Lambda56=-lambda*tau/2 could not be nonnegative for tau>0.

Thus EVERY base remainder is0. At t=0, (19)--(20) also give Delta=0,
so the right child is unique even among the allowed signed-terminal
arrays. For the left child the incoming-first-node mask
Lambda20=Lambda30=0 follows from(14); its remainder is0, and the same
calculation gives uniqueness. With the forced cross coefficients,

 (hbar,Lambda feasible) ==> Lambda=Lambdabar and M=0.        (21)

This UNVERIFIED pointwise base result is not a neighborhood theorem.

## 5. Uniform control of arbitrary nearby certificate selections

Choose a small closed neighborhood with all steps in[1,4].
The scalar quadratic f(x)=k*x^2/2 with k=1/8 and x0=1 has factors
1-k*h_i in[1/2,7/8], so its positions strictly decrease. Every distinct
pair has a uniformly positive separation. Its exact residual is

 Q_ij=-k(1-k)(x_i-x_j)^2/2<0.                              (22)

Compactness and finitely many pairs give -Q_ij>=delta>0 uniformly.
This quadratic's V_h is continuous and bounded above by a finite B.
Identity(2) for any nearby feasible array gives

 delta*sum Lambda_ij <= V_h <= B.                          (23)

Thus ALL such arrays are uniformly bounded. If h_n->hbar, any subsequential
limit of its arrays is feasible at hbar; (21) identifies it as Lambdabar.
A compactness contradiction therefore proves Lambda->Lambdabar uniformly
over arbitrary selections.

Only convergence has been proved. An O(||h-hbar||) rate has NOT been
proved; an unbounded coefficient difference quotient remains possible.

## 6. Six necessary PHYSICAL tangent inequalities

Let p in R7 be any contingent physical direction: t_n>0 tends to0 and
feasible words have h_n=hbar+t_n*p+o(t_n), with arbitrary full arrays.
Write S=sum p_i, ell=p0+p1+p2, r=p4+p5+p6, alpha=u^3.
The following SIX inequalities are necessary:

 JQ=alpha*S+lambda*(p0+p2+p4+p6)+p1+p5+u*p3 <=0,
 JC=alpha*S+u*p3-u^2*(ell+r) <=0,
 JL(1)<=0, JL(-1)<=0, JR(1)<=0, JR(-1)<=0,                  (24)

where

 JL(theta)=alpha*S-u*p0+p1+(1+q*theta)*p2
                       +u*p3+lambda*p4+p5+lambda*p6,
 JR(theta)=alpha*S-u^2*ell+u*p3
                       +(1+q*theta)*p4+p5+lambda*p6.       (25)

Denote the cone defined EXACTLY by these inequalities C_stat47. It is
the exact surviving space of THESE derived contact tests, not asserted
to be the physical feasible tangent cone.

The unit quadratic gives JQ by differentiating log(A*|v7|).
The central cut gives JC: for h3=1+d and side capacities
P=1+h0+h1+h2,R=1+h4+h5+h6, a clipped interval witness starts at P,
ends with magnitude d/R, and has energy P^2-A^2*d^2/R^2.
Thus A*d<=P*R; its logarithmic derivative is JC.

For JR fix theta in(-1,1). Near hbar set
P=1+h0+h1+h2,d=h3-1,a=h4,B=h5-1,C=h6-1, and

 R2=[(a-1)d^2-d(2-a^2)theta]/(a+1),
 U=(1,0), V=(theta,sqrt(R2-theta^2)), G=-(dU+V)/a,
 K=conv{U,G,V,-B*V}.                                      (26)

For sufficiently small changes R2>theta^2 and 0<BC<1. Start at P*U.
Gradients are U through3, then G,V,-B*V,BC*V. All GD equations hold.
Only prefix normals and y4=(-(a-1)dU+V)/a can be nonzero.
The definition of R2 is precisely y4 dot(V-G)=0. Every other nontrivial
vertex comparison is strictly negative at hbar by Section2(c), and stays
so locally for fixed theta. The other normals vanish. This gives an
ACTUAL static function, unique minimum0, all rows, and true radius P.
Its energy is P^2-A^2 B^2 C^2 R2. At the base,

 (R2)'=2u*p3+2(1+q*theta)*p4.

The log derivative of A*B*C*sqrt(R2)/P is JR(theta).
All open theta are allowed, so their endpoint limits give JR(+/-1).
No degenerate endpoint quadrilateral is asserted legal.

For JL the direct continuation need not be an actual trajectory. This
issue is handled explicitly. For fixed theta in(-1,1) put
k=u*theta,d=h1-1,a=h2,P=1+h0 and

 R2=[(a-1)d^2-d(2-a^2)k]/(a+1),
 U=(1,0),V=(k,sqrt(R2-k^2)),G=-(dU+V)/a.                    (27)

Use formal x0=P*U,g0=g1=U,x2=-dU,g2=G,x3=g3=V.
From3 onward set g_i=x_i and x_(i+1)=(1-h_i)x_i.
Set f_i=x_i dot g_i-||g_i||^2/2. These formal jets are analytic near
hbar and are exactly the ACTUAL witness of Section2(d) at the base.
Only y0=h0 U and y2=(-(a-1)dU+V)/a can be nonzero, with
y2 dot(V-G)=0.

EVERY positive Lambdabar entry has Q_ij=0 identically along this formal
continuation. The only positive target0 entry is10, and the only positive
target2 entry is32. All other positive entries target zero normals.
An entry with a strict negative base residual has a nonpositive
contribution Lambda_ij Q_ij near hbar. For a zero base residual with
Lambdabar_ij=0, (21)--(23) give Lambda_ij=o(1) uniformly, while its
analytic residual is O(||h-hbar||). The sum of positive contributions
is therefore o(||h-hbar||). Apply the FORMAL identity(2) and full PSD:

 V_h >= -o(||h-hbar||).                                   (28)

No legality of the continued jets or differentiability of the arrays
was assumed. The right terminal product is
(h3-1)(h4-1)(h5-1)(h6-1), so the formal energy is

 P^2-A^2[(h3-1)(h4-1)(h5-1)(h6-1)]^2 R2.

At the base R2=u^2 and (R2)'/(2u^2)=p1+(1+q*theta)p2.
The first-order limit of(28) gives JL(theta)<=0 and hence its two endpoint
inequalities. Ignoring the return to V at query5 would have falsely
asserted a finite actual-function obstruction for(27).

The other global cuts are strictly slack at hbar. Their exact margins
P_j*R_j-A(h_j-1), for j=0,1,2,4,5,6, are
4+2q,6+4q,18+12q,18+12q,6+4q,4+2q. They add no first-order restriction.
The earlier final-pair defect is also strictly slack here. With
P=1+sum_(i<5)hbar_i=5+4q, B=hbar5-1=1,C=hbar6-1=u,

 H_pair=BC[1+B(P+Abar)/P^2]-1=-4*Abar/P^2<0.                 (29)

Indeed P^2=57+40q and u[P^2+P+Abar]=29+20q.
Its triangle witness has positive energy at hbar, so it supplies no
equality tangent here. Its possible force farther away is not discarded.

## 7. A surviving ascent vector and the ENTIRE lifted first-order problem

The claim that(24) alone excludes ascent is false. Take

 p_test=(0,0,0,0,0,2,-1), S=1.                              (30)

JQ and all four JL/JR endpoint values equal
u^3+2-lambda=u^3-u<0. JC=u^3-u^2<0. Thus p_test lies strictly inside
C_stat47 and has positive capacity derivative. This is a counterexample
to sufficiency of the CONTACT TESTS for a no-ascent conclusion.
It is not a claimed feasible physical path or feasible lifted tangent.

Here is the full lifted first-order system, leaving all seven physical
coordinates independent. Let Y be ANY zero-diagonal real8-by8 array,
F=L(Y), T0=T(hbar), D0=D(hbar), Lbar=L(Lambdabar), and

 T_p,ij=p_j for j<i, zero otherwise,
 omega_p=(-2p0,...,-2p6,2S)^T,
 D1(p)=diag(2(1-hbar0)p0,...,2(1-hbar6)p6,-2*Abar*S),
 N(p)=D1(p)+sym(Lbar*T_p),
 W=N(p)+sym(F*T0).                                         (31)

Original flow, signs and PSD linearize to

 F^T e=omega_p,
 Y_ij>=0 at ALL THIRTY-SIX zero entries of Lambdabar,
 W>=0 on the ENTIRE R8.                                    (32)

The other twenty derivative entries are unrestricted real numbers;
their finite coefficients must still be nonnegative.
Under this flow e^T W e=0; PSD therefore gives W e=0. The other forced
moment is

 vbar^T W vbar=chi1(p)=-2JQ.                                (33)

It follows by differentiating v(h)^T M(h)v(h)=chi(h), using Mbar=0.
Denote the projection of(32) onto p by C_lin47. This is the exact
surviving space for the ORIGINAL LINEARIZED tests. This semidefinite
projection has NOT been solved or eliminated, and equality with the
physical contingent cone is NOT asserted.

A concrete direct completion attempt for(30) goes as follows.
Its flow increment is(0,0,0,0,0,-4,2,2)^T; D1 has diagonal
(0,0,0,0,0,-4,2u,-2lambda^3). T_p has only entries
T_p,65=T_p,75=2,T_p,76=-1. Thus N is explicit from the twenty-entry table.
Here chi1=2(u-u^3)>0. Let P0=I-ee^T/8, nu=vbar^T P0 vbar>0, and

 W_test=(chi1/nu)*P0.                                      (34)

It is genuinely PSD on R8, positive definite on e-perp, with both
required moments. This solves the FULL PSD/moment portion of the
attempt, but not the original coefficient signs.

Every REAL flow completion for this attempted W_test can be displayed.
Put R0=T0^-1,E1=W_test-N(p_test),mu=e^T vbar/8,w=vbar-mu e,nu=w^T w,

 a1=E1*e-T0^T*omega_p, b1=-E1*vbar,
 c1=b1-mu*a1, d1=c1+e*(a1^T w)/8,
 K1=(a1*e^T-e*a1^T)/8+(d1*w^T-w*d1^T)/nu,
 F=(E1+K1+J)*R0, J^T=-J, J*e=J*vbar=0,
 Y_ij=-F_ij for i!=j, Y_ii=0.                              (35)

The compatibility identities are
e^T a1=0,vbar^T b1=0,e^T b1+vbar^T a1=0.
For the first, differentiate e^T D e+omega^T T e=0 to get
e^T N e=-omega_p^T T0 e. For the second, vbar^T N vbar=chi1:
differentiate the calibrated moment, or insert
v'=-R0*T_p*vbar in v^T Dv and use
D0*vbar=-T0^T*Lbar^T*vbar/2. The mixed identity uses
T0*vbar=e and e^T omega_p=0.
These identities give e^T d1=w^T d1=0, K1e=a1,K1vbar=b1.
Consequently F e=0,F^T e=omega_p,sym(F*T0)=E1, as required.
Conversely any two skew completions differ by a skew killing e,vbar.
Thus(35) covers every real completion. Its homogeneous J has fifteen
free entries on the six-dimensional orthogonal complement. There is no
implicit matrix solve in R0: it has diagonal1, zero upper entries and
R0_ij=-hbar_j*product_(j<k<i)(1-hbar_k) for i>j. Forward substitution
in T0*x=e_j proves this formula: x_(j+1)=-hbar_j and each later value
is multiplied by 1-hbar_i.

For this attempted W_test the FIRST unsolved sign calculation is exactly

 [(E1+K1+J)*R0]_ij<=0
      for every off-diagonal ij absent from the table.     (36)

For example, put B1=E1+K1+J. The actual missing entries56 and04 require
B1_56-q*B1_57<=0 and
B1_04-q*B1_05+q*B1_06-q*u*B1_07<=0, respectively. In the first row,
N56=lambda^3+2lambda+2lambda*q+2lambda^3*q and
N57=2lambda+2lambda^3, so the non-skew contribution is exactly
E1_56-q*E1_57=u*chi1/(8nu)-lambda^3-2lambda.
These are examples from the same full list, not a replacement for it.
The thirty-six inequalities are linear in the unrestricted skew.
No claim that(36) is feasible or infeasible is made.
Choosing W_test was an attempt, not a lossless rank or shape restriction.
The full problem allows EVERY W>=0 with We=0 and(33), and uses the same
formula with E1=W-N(p). This recovers(32) with all original signs.

## 8. Exact quadratic corrections and a sufficient integration interface

A solution of(32) need not integrate. For arbitrary finite increments
p=h-hbar,Y=Lambda-Lambdabar,S=sum p_i, define

 D2(p)=diag(-p0^2,...,-p6^2,-S^2).

The EXACT full remainder, without neglected terms, is

 M=N(p)+sym(L(Y)*T0)+D2(p)+sym(L(Y)*T_p).                    (37)

Flow is exactly L(Y)^T e=omega_p. Exact signs are Y_ij>=0 at the
thirty-six zeros, and Lambdabar_ij+Y_ij>=0 at the other twenty.
Together with full PSD, (37) includes EVERY nearby word and array,
including lifts with unbounded difference quotients.

For expansions h=hbar+t*p+t^2*b+o(t^2),
Lambda=Lambdabar+t*Y+t^2*Z+o(t^2), the second remainder is

 W2=N(b)+sym(L(Z)*T0)+D2(p)+sym(L(Y)*T_p).                   (38)

Second flow is L(Z)^T e=omega_b. At a zero base entry with Y_ij=0,
sign feasibility requires Z_ij>=0. Also W2 e=0 is necessary.
If W is singular, W2 restricted to ker W must be PSD. That alone
does not ensure a truncated candidate is feasible. On e-perp decompose
range(W) plus ker(W), and write

 W=[A 0;0 0], W2=[B C;C^T D], A positive definite.

For M/t=W+tW2+O(t^2), completing the square in the upper variable
gives a lower Schur complement with leading terms

 tD-t^2 C^T A^-1 C + the lower O(t^2) term.                 (39)

The upper inverse is A^-1+O(t). A zero direction of D with an unpaid
mixed block can therefore fail at the next order; higher corrections
matter. The same issue occurs at zero leading multiplier signs.
For p_test specifically,

 D2(p_test)=diag(0,0,0,0,0,-4,-1,-1).                       (40)

An affine Lambda candidate must pay this actual negative diagonal and
the mixed term sym(L(Y)T_p). The positive moment matrix(34) alone
does not do so.

There IS a precise sufficient first-order target. Suppose(32) has
S>0, W positive definite on e-perp, and Y_ij>0 at ALL thirty-six zero
base entries. Then there is an analytic full certificate with
A>lambda^3 for all sufficiently small positive t. Here is a constructive
proof, conditional on that explicitly missing strict solution.

Set h(t)=hbar+t*p,v(t)=T(h(t))^-1 e, and

 M(t)=chi(h(t))*W/[v(t)^T W v(t)].                          (41)

The denominator at0 is chi1>0, and chi has derivative chi1.
Thus M(t)=tW+O(t^2), is PSD for small t>0, kills e exactly and has
the exact calibrated moment. For each t define
mu=e^T v/8,w=v-mu e,nu=||w||^2, and

 a=-D(h)e-T(h)^T omega(h), b=(D(h)-M(t))v,
 c=b-mu a, d=c+e*(a^T w)/8,
 K0(t)=(a e^T-e a^T)/8+(d w^T-w d^T)/nu.

All denominators stay nonzero. The identities
e^T D e+omega^T T e=0, v^T Dv=chi, and the moment of M imply the same
compatibilities as in(35), hence K0e=a,K0v=b exactly.
Let P(t)=I-ee^T/8-w(t)w(t)^T/nu(t), the analytic orthogonal projection
onto the complement of e,v(t).

Write Kbar=skew(Lbar*T0), Kdot=skew(L(Y)*T0+Lbar*T_p),
Jbar=Kbar-K0(0),Jdot=Kdot-K0'(0). Differentiating their equations gives
Jdot e=0,Jdot vbar+Jbar v'(0)=0. Since J=PJP,

 Jdot=P'(0)Jbar+Jbar P'(0)+P(0)Jdot P(0).

Therefore the skew

 J(t)=P(t)[Jbar+t*P(0)Jdot P(0)]P(t)

has value Jbar and derivative Jdot, and kills e,v(t) exactly.
Set

 L(t)=[M(t)-D(h(t))+K0(t)+J(t)]T(h(t))^-1,
 Lambda_ij(t)=-L_ij(t) off diagonal, Lambda_ii(t)=0.         (42)

Multiplication gives L(t)e=0,L(t)^T e=omega(h(t)) and the exact M(t).
Its value and derivative are Lambdabar,Y. Strict derivative signs make
all thirty-six formerly zero entries positive for small t>0; the twenty
positive entries remain so by continuity. Finally A=lambda^3+tS.
This proves the conditional integration statement with every original
check. No such strict Y,W has been found in this round.

If that strict system fails, singular W and zero first derivatives
remain possible and require(38)--(39) or higher-order work.
Failure at one chosen W/skew is not a neighborhood theorem.
Even absence of first-order capacity gain would not exclude S=0 with
sum b_i>0, or a lift with unbounded difference quotient.

## 9. Handoff and exact scope

Completed at JOINT UNVERIFIED draft level:

* Actual static equality witnesses, full directed normal checks,
  unique minima, true radii and the original eight formal coordinates.
* All-base-array rigidity: remainder ray(13), elimination by Lambda56,
  and derived full uniqueness(21), without an assumed representative.
* Uniform boundedness and convergence of arbitrary nearby arrays.
* Six explicit necessary physical tangent tests(24), handling the
  potentially illegal left continuation through(28).
* Exact slack of the older final-pair defect at the balanced point.
* A positive-capacity vector strictly surviving these contacts, its full
  PSD/moment completion, and the remaining original sign system(36).
* The full first-order system(32), exact finite quadratic model(37),
  higher-order cautions, and a conditional strict integration construction.

The FIRST unproved constructive interface is solving(32) with S>0 and
sufficient strictness for(41)--(42), or solving its singular higher-order
version. The missing negative interface is a bound A(h)<=lambda^3 on
an ACTUAL full neighborhood, covering every full certificate, zero
first-order capacity changes and nonsmooth coefficient lifts.
Neither is established. C_stat47 is an explicit contact relaxation;
C_lin47 is the explicit unresolved full linearized projection.
Neither has been identified with the physical feasible tangent cone.

No arbitrary new one-dimensional path was screened for a quick
counterexample. The vector(30) diagnoses the actual seven-coordinate
contact cone. No global seven-step, other-length or original-target
impossibility follows. Accepted5e1848ee0b153f5f would amplify an ACTUAL
above-threshold certificate; the contact vector, moment matrix and
conditional integration statement supply no such certificate.

Literature process: Matlas returned the strict-kernel perturbation
lemma of Neeman--Paouris, arXiv1605.07233, Lemma3.2. The exact PDF was
downloaded and its web-extracted statement/proof read. Its strict
positivity hypothesis does not settle the zero directions here; no
external theorem is a proof input. Local pdftotext was unavailable,
exit127; no installation was attempted. Schur and reconstruction
calculations needed here are proved explicitly. A second Matlas query
for local Silver optimality returned unrelated results; web search
returned already known Silver/composability sources, with no applicable
local full-cone theorem imported.

ZERO numerical evaluations, solvers, parameter searches, symbolic
evaluation programs, benchmark replays, formal calls, installations,
consult calls or subagents were used. Text/file operations and literature
retrieval are administrative only. The one allocated round ends with
this incomplete proof status; no extension or process-exit claim is made.

# Frozen full tangent model — max2, round48

Status before job1: joint UNVERIFIED mathematics. No formal gate authorized.
Source: shared b937bb05ba1645b1, SHA256
76a363742a60cdab32d14b828dcf7ecd58d784dfcededfd1b9510b131d67565b.
Accepted input: 04219554e41d9750, Sections10--11 (original identity and
full primitive joins). The source draft is not an accepted premise; the
identities required here are derived below. Original target remains open.

Write q=sqrt(2), l=1+q, u=q-1. The exact base is
h=(q,2,q,1+l,q,2,q), A=l^3, with the twenty entries in source Section1.
All56 ordered pairs are retained, in row-major order excluding diagonal.
Let e be the all-ones column of length8. T has unit diagonal and T_ij=h_j
for j<i; L(X)=diag(Xe)-X. For p in R7, S=sum p, put
Tp_ij=p_j for j<i (zero otherwise), op=(-2p,2S),
D1=diag(2(1-h_i)p_i for i<7,-2AS),
W=D1+sym(Lbar Tp+L(Y)T). Flow is L(Y)^T e=op.
All36 off-diagonal entries absent from the positive base table obey Y>=0.
Other twenty Y entries are arbitrary real. W is the full original R8
matrix, with no rank, support, reflection or equality-of-gradients ansatz.

For any h and full flow, e^T M e=sum h_i(2-h_i)-s(s+2)+omega^T Te=0:
omega^T Te=-2sum h_i(1+sum_(j<i)h_j)+2s(1+s)
=s^2+sum h_i^2. Differentiating along exact affine flow proves e^TWe=0.
For symmetric PSD W, testing e+t z proves We=0. Conversely, with We=0,
take V=[I7;-1...-1], G=V^TV=I7+11^T. Every vector is a sum of a multiple
of e and Vz. Hence W>=0 iff B=V^TWV>=0. Also W>=m(I-ee^T/8) iff B>=mG.
Thus imposing all8 We equations and B>=0 is lossless for the full cone.
Every output is read back using the original eight coordinates.

Positive calibration, established before numerical evaluation:
for x=q-t with 0<=t<q, the singleton has multipliers2,2(1+x), flow(-2x,2x)
and remainder(2-x^2)[[1,-1],[-1,1]]. Join identical singleton children
with bridge1+u(1+x), and identical three-step children with bridge2+x.
The child capacities are1+x,l(1+x),l^2(1+x), respectively. Each join has
right factor k=l^2 and additional multiplier factor k-1=2l. The accepted
full join identity gives a genuine feasible word. Its full array has:
01=2;10=2(1+x);13=2(1+x);21=2lx;23=2l^2+2lx;31=2l;
32=2l^2(1+x);37=2l(1+x);43=2lx;45=2l^2;47=2lx;
53=2l+2(1+x);54=2l^2(1+x);57=2l^2(1+x)+2l+2(1+x);
63=2lx;65=2l^3x;67=2l^4+2l^3x+2lx;73=2l;75=2l^3;
76=2l^4(1+x). All other entries zero. At t=0 this is exactly Lambdabar.
Consequently p=(-1,-u,-1,-1,-1,-u,-1), S=-l^2, and Y is the negative
x-derivative of that entire array. If d_ij is the coordinate difference
vector e_i-e_j, its remainder derivative is exactly
W=2q[d01d01^T+l^2 d23d23^T+l^2 d45d45^T+l^4 d67d67^T].
This is nonzero and PSD, with every required zero-entry derivative zero.

Negative calibration, established before numerical evaluation:
v(h)=T(h)^(-1)e has v_0=1,v_(i+1)=(1-h_i)v_i. Since L(Y)e=0,
v^T Mv=v^T Dv=1-A(h)^2 v_7^2 for any zero-diagonal array.
At the supplied base M=0 and A^2v_7^2=1, so differentiation gives
vbar^TWvbar=-2[S/l^3+l(p0+p2+p4+p6)+p1+p5+up3].
For p=(1,...,1) this is -2[7/l^3+4l+2+u]<0, contradicting full PSD.

Frozen optimization models and branch rule:
1. Known nonzero calibration: direct exact Q(sqrt2) original-array checks
   and one feasibility solve with p and all56 Y fixed to the known object.
2. Negative calibration: full cone with p_i=1, all56 Y free, objective0.
   Require numerical infeasibility and a credible complete dual ray.
3. Free-p strict model: |p_i|<=1, maximize m, S>=m, all36 Y_zero>=m,
   flow=0, We=0, V^TWV>=mG. No bounds on Y or W. No imposed m>=0.
4. If job3 has status Solved/AlmostSolved, all original equality residuals
   <=1e-7, and m,S,min(Y_zero),min_eperp(W)>=1e-5, reconstruct that
   strict primal exactly. Otherwise maximize S over the same |p_i|<=1
   and full nonstrict cone (no m variable).
5. If job4 was an SDP: if it has credible positive S>1e-5, attempt one
   exact primal reconstruction; otherwise attempt one exact homogeneous
   dual reconstruction with all p-box prices set exactly zero. If job4
   was reconstruction and succeeded, retire job5. If reconstruction
   failed, use job5 only for independent original-array readback of the
   selected raw object. No extra optimization call. A documented code
   correction can instead consume job5; no automatic retry.
Calibration failure closes unused jobs. A solver label or small residual
is not proof. Exact reconstruction failures remain failures, with arrays
preserved. No transfer from older budgets; stop at reconstruction.

Exact reconstruction: round free numerical coordinates to the nearest
multiple of2^-20, ties to even. Solve the exact affine equations over

Y before p, retain all16 flow/null equations, then check every original
sign and full B by exact LDL with guarded zero pivots. For a dual, use

unrestricted-Y stationarity rows and7 p rows, and derive all36 sign
prices from stationarity. All box prices are zero as a reconstruction
ansatz, never a search restriction. Check exact stationarity, every sign
price and exact PSD of the7x7 dual matrix. This can establish only S<=0
in the linearized cone, not neighborhood or global optimality.

Numerics: existing public run_shared_math_python.sh; NumPy/scipy/Clarabel
only, no installation or alternate runtime. IEEE float64. Clarabel
max_iter150,time_limit4s,tol_gap_abs=tol_gap_rel=tol_feas=
tol_infeas_abs=tol_infeas_rel=1e-9. Other defaults preserved; serialize
settings. PSD encoding is column-major upper svec with sqrt2 off-diagonal

bound8s includes imports,build,solve,readback and writes. Reserve8s before
launch, count all failures/timeouts, never refund. Single-thread numerical

hashing are administrative and have separately measured time.

Raw data: freeze source hashes, save full dense conic A,b,q and index map;
save raw x,s,z,status,objectives,iterations,solver times,logs/warnings before
interpretation. Export all56 Y,all36 signs,eight flow residuals,full W,
We,symmetry residual,complement eigenvalues,dual PSD and stationarity,

# Full seven-step tangent discovery: exact dual candidate

**Status: complete joint UNVERIFIED research report.** All five authorized
jobs are consumed and the ledger is closed. There was no fact submission,
formal verification, installation, benchmark replay or subagent. The original
unrestricted gradient-descent exact-profile problem remains OPEN.

The useful output is an explicit dual over Q(sqrt(2)) for the COMPLETE
first-order cone at the frozen balanced seven-step word. The recorded exact
reconstruction satisfies all63 stationarity equations, all36 sign-price
inequalities, and an exact positive-definite LDL decomposition. It gives
the following candidate conclusion:

> Every full linearized certificate (p,Y,W) satisfies sum_i p_i <= 0.
> This conclusion covers all seven physical derivatives, all56 original
> coefficient derivatives, all36 original active signs and full R8 PSD.

This is a proof candidate with explicit algebraic evidence, not an accepted
fact. No full-neighborhood, global seven-step or original-target theorem is
claimed. In particular the discovery supplies no improving finite seed.

## 1. Inputs, exact model and scope

The active assignment is TASK.md, ONE round after completed47, completed
ceiling48/attempted49, under guidance06cd713a43322da8. The shared preceding
draft b937bb05ba1645b1 has SHA256
76a363742a60cdab32d14b828dcf7ecd58d784dfcededfd1b9510b131d67565b.
It identifies this exact cone but does not solve it. Its base uniqueness,
contact and integration claims are not imported as accepted facts here.

Accepted04219554e41d9750, Sections10--11, supplies the original full
primitive identity and composition. Accepted1252b8823bb5a290 fixes the
interpolation orientation. Accepted5e1848ee0b153f5f supplies conditional
amplification of an actual above-threshold seed only. None asserts such
a seed exists. All additional identities required below are derived jointly.

Let q=sqrt(2), l=1+q, u=q-1, and

    hbar=(q,2,q,1+l,q,2,q), Abar=l^3.

Index the eight independent formal gradient coordinates by0,...,7. Let
e be their all-ones column and let e_i be the standard coordinate columns.
For any zero-diagonal8-by8 array X, put L(X)=diag(Xe)-X. Define T0 to have
unit diagonal, entry hbar_j at i,j when j<i, and zero entries above its
diagonal. The base array has these twenty positive entries, with every
unlisted entry zero:

    01:2                  10:2l                 13:2l
    21:2lq                23:2l^2+2lq           31:2l
    32:2l^3               37:2l^2               43:2lq
    45:2l^2               47:2lq                53:4l
    54:2l^3               57:2l^3+4l            63:2lq
    65:2l^3q              67:2l^4+2l^3q+2lq
    73:2l                 75:2l^3               76:2l^5.

Call this array Lambdabar and write Lbar=L(Lambdabar). Let Z0 be the
set of the other36 ordered distinct pairs. The complete variable array
Y has Y_ii=0 and all56 other entries independently real before constraints.
For p in R7 set S=sum p_i and define

    Tp_ij=p_j for j<i, zero otherwise;
    op=(-2p_0,...,-2p_6,2S);
    D1=diag(2(1-hbar_i)p_i for i<7,-2Abar S);
    W=D1+sym(Lbar Tp+L(Y)T0), sym(K)=(K+K^T)/2;
    f=L(Y)^T e-op, n=We.

The full cone consists of f=0, Y_ij>=0 for ij in Z0 and W>=0 on R8.
The other twenty Y entries have no imposed sign. No support, rank,
reflection, equality of formal gradients or fixed shape of W is assumed.

## 2. Exact common null and lossless PSD representation

For an arbitrary word h, s=sum h_i, and full flow L^Te=(-2h,2s), put
M=D+sym(LT), D=diag(h_i(2-h_i),-s(s+2)), with T the original prefix
matrix. Since the i-th entry of Te is1+sum_(j<i)h_j,

    omega^T Te=s^2+sum h_i^2,
    e^T D e=-s^2-sum h_i^2,

so e^TMe=0. Along hbar+t p and Lambdabar+tY, the flow equation is
exact whenever f=0, and differentiation gives e^TWe=0. A symmetric PSD
matrix with zero quadratic form at e annihilates e: its quadratic form
at e+t z cannot be nonnegative for all real t unless z^TWe=0 for each z.
Thus n=0 is a necessary full-cone equation.

Let V=[I7;-1...-1], G=V^TV=I7+11^T and B=V^TWV. Its columns span
e-perp. When n=0, every vector is a sum of a multiple of e and a vector
Vz, so W>=0 on R8 if and only if B>=0 on R7. Moreover

    W>=m(I8-ee^T/8)  iff  B>=mG.

Consequently adding all8 n=0 equations and imposing this congruent PSD
condition loses no original solution. The common-null direction never
receives an epsilon perturbation. Raw readbacks contain the original
full W, every original flow equation, and the full null residual.

## 3. Both calibrations established before job1

For the singleton physical step x, choose multipliers2 and2(1+x) on the
two directed pairs. Substitution into the original two-gradient identity
gives the full remainder

    (2-x^2)(e_0-e_1)(e_0-e_1)^T.

It is nonnegative when0<=x<=q. Set x=q-t,0<=t<q. Join identical
singleton children using bridge1+u(1+x), then identical three-step
children using bridge2+x. Their respective capacities are1+x,l(1+x),
and l^2(1+x). Each balanced join has right multiplier factor l^2 and
additional interpolation factor l^2-1=2l. The accepted full join identity
therefore proves feasibility without a computation. The complete array
and its derivative are specified in frozen_model.md and in the exact
calibration artifact.

The derivative is p=(-1,-u,-1,-1,-1,-u,-1), with S=-l^2. If
d_ij=e_i-e_j, its exact remainder derivative is

    W=2q(d01 d01^T+l^2 d23 d23^T+l^2 d45 d45^T+l^4 d67 d67^T).

Every active zero-entry derivative is zero. This is a NONZERO positive
calibration from an actual feasible path. Job1 recorded exact base flow,
base M=0, derivative flow, null, all36 signs and the displayed rank-one
sum identity, then numerically solved the full fixed calibration.

For the excluded direction, v(h)=T(h)^(-1)e obeys v_0=1 and
v_(i+1)=(1-h_i)v_i. Since L e=0,

    v^T Mv=v^TDv=1-A(h)^2 v_7^2.

At the base M=0 and Abar^2 vbar_7^2=1. Differentiating yields

    vbar^T W vbar=-2[S/l^3+l(p0+p2+p4+p6)+p1+p5+up3].

The direction p=(1,...,1) has strictly negative right side
-2(7/l^3+4l+2+u), so it cannot satisfy full PSD for any Y. This analytic
contradiction was frozen before the negative numerical calibration.

## 4. Frozen jobs and observations

Before any mathematical import or evaluation, frozen_model.md,
tangent_jobs.py and run_job.py were hashed in freeze.json. The numerical
implementation uses the already published shared Python runtime and direct
Clarabel cone data. The [official Clarabel SDP example](https://clarabel.org/stable/examples/py/example_sdp/)
specifies the upper-triangular, column-major svec convention, with q on
off-diagonal entries. The [Python interface documentation](https://clarabel.org/stable/python/getting_started_py/)
specifies the complete primal/dual return arrays. These are implementation
sources; no external mathematical theorem is used as a proof premise.

All p coordinates are bounded by |p_i|<=1 during the two discovery SDPs.
No other variable is bounded. The strict problem maximizes m subject to
S>=m, all36 Y_zero>=m, f=n=0 and B>=mG. If the frozen credibility test
fails, the nonstrict problem maximizes S subject to the complete cone.
All options, thresholds and reconstruction choices are in the freeze.

|Job|Action|Recorded status/result|External process seconds|
|---|---|---|---|
|1|Exact nonzero calibration and feasibility SDP|Exact identities passed; `AlmostSolved`; original equality residual3.893774191965349e-12|2.8775796109985095|
|2|Excluded p=(1,...,1) calibration SDP|`PrimalInfeasible`; complete dual ray passed frozen readback|1.7735932099894853|
|3|Free-p strict-margin SDP|`AlmostSolved`; m=-1.7373600391814525e-9|1.7222137329954421|
|4|Free-p nonstrict capacity SDP|`AlmostSolved`; S=-5.056399669088169e-8|1.722976656004903|
|5|One exact homogeneous dual reconstruction|All63 exact stationarity residuals zero;36 sign prices nonnegative; exact dual LDL positive definite|0.36915727599989623|

The job3 numerical gap was2.547742288815272e-9; its smallest recorded
complement eigenvalue was-1.420580825145002e-9. It does not supply a
strict tangent. Job4's numerical gap was7.87119183312904e-8; its original
equality residual was8.644757023430468e-13. Neither of these numerical
outcomes by itself proves a bound. Their raw dual object was only a lead
for the exact reconstruction in Section5.

All five jobs returned process code0; no timeout or repair occurred.
`AlmostSolved` is a reduced-accuracy status, preserved without relabeling.
The positive calibration's acceptance follows the frozen tolerance rule
and independent exact calibration formulas, not a claim of exactness for
the floating solver point. The negative calibration also had its exact
moment contradiction before solving.

Each job was charged the FULL8 seconds before launch and externally
limited to8 seconds, covering imports, model construction, solve,
readback and writes. Total accounted usage is40 seconds. Total measured
external process time is8.465520485988236 seconds. Internal solver and
kernel timers and administrative times are recorded separately in raw
files and the ledger. No unmeasured transport time is presented as kernel
time. No credit is refunded or transferred. All remaining jobs are zero.

All original primal and dual arrays, dense conic data, index maps,
settings, stdout/stderr, statuses, gaps, eigendiagnostics and return codes

## 5. The exact dual and the candidate proof

Job5 rounded free coordinates once to multiples of2^-20 and solved exact
affine equations in the field Q(q), q^2=2. All p-box prices were set to
zero as a reconstruction ansatz. This was not a restriction of either SDP.
No optimization or parameter grid was used in reconstruction.

The appendix specifies two eight-coordinate vectors a,b (the first and
last halves of `equality_prices`), a symmetric7-by7 matrix Z through its
28 svec entries, and36 coefficients sigma_ij. All their displayed numbers
are exact rational pairs (r,s), meaning r+s q. Off-diagonal svec entries
are q Z_ij, and diagonal entries are Z_ii.

The algebraic identity certified by the63 zero stationarity residuals is

    -S = trace(Z B)+sum_(ij in Z0) sigma_ij Y_ij
         -a^T f-b^T n.                                      (D)

This is an identity for EVERY p and EVERY zero-diagonal real Y, before
any cone constraint. There is no p-normalization term because all14 box
prices are exactly zero.

Here is an explicit way to check every coefficient of(D) without a
solver. Set H=VZV^T and K=H-sym(b e^T). Write t_i for the transpose of
the i-th row of T0 and r_ij=t_i-t_j. A single unit Y_ij contributes
e_i-e_j to f and sym(e_i r_ij^T) to W. Thus the56 required identities are

    a_i-a_j-(K r_ij)_i = 0              on the20 base-positive pairs,
    a_i-a_j-(K r_ij)_i = sigma_ij       on the36 other pairs. (Y)

For each i<7 let C_i have entry1 in column i strictly below its diagonal,
and zero elsewhere. The unit p_i contribution to W is

    N_i=2(1-hbar_i)e_i e_i^T-2Abar e_7e_7^T+sym(Lbar C_i).

Its contribution to f is2e_i-2e_7. The seven remaining identities are

    2(a_i-a_7)-trace(K N_i)=1, i=0,...,6.                  (P)

The appendixed numbers and q^2=2 specify all entries in(Y) and(P).
The reconstruction artifact records each of their63 residuals as the
exact pair(0,0), using the equivalent unsimplified original coefficient
map. It retains the exact source and deterministic arithmetic procedure.

All36 sigma values are nonnegative in the real embedding q>0, and none
is zero. The following elementary rule makes their comparisons exact:
if r,s have the same sign, use that sign; if they have opposite signs,
the sign of r+s q is sign(r) times sign(r^2-2s^2). The cases r=0 or
s=0 are immediate. The nonzero opposite-sign case cannot yield zero,
because q is irrational. This is the precise rule used by the frozen
exact arithmetic, with rational operations only.

For Z, the artifact supplies Z=L diag(d_0,...,d_6)L^T with unit lower
triangular L and all seven pivots d_i strictly positive under that same
sign rule. The appendix includes L and all seven exact pairs. The LDL
recurrence is

    d_k=Z_kk-sum_(j<k)L_kj^2 d_j,
    L_ik=(Z_ik-sum_(j<k)L_ij L_kj d_j)/d_k, i>k.

These formulas prove that the supplied decomposition entails Z>0, with
no floating eigenvalue inference. All pivots were tested exactly before
division, and a zero pivot with nonzero remaining column would have been
rejected. The recorded result has no zero pivot.

For a full feasible tangent, f=n=0, every Y_ij in Z0 is nonnegative and
B>=0. If Z=L diag(d)L^T, then
trace(ZB)=sum_k d_k (L e_k)^T B(L e_k)>=0. Applying(D) gives -S>=0.
This proves the candidate first-order capacity bound from the explicit
algebraic checks. A zero tangent attains S=0, so the normalized nonstrict
problem has exact optimum0 at draft level. The strict model likewise has
optimum0: S>=m and S<=0 imply m<=0, and the zero point with m=0 is feasible.

The same identity also gives a useful equality condition: if S=0, each
active Y entry is zero, and B=0 because Z is positive definite. To see
the latter without a spectral theorem, each nonnegative summand
(L e_k)^T B(L e_k) must vanish; PSD then makes B annihilate the basis
formed by the columns of L. Consequently W=0 as well. This does not
by itself settle how a finite higher-order perturbation can behave.

## 6. What this closes and what remains

At candidate level this resolves the specific missing lifted tangent
interface in b937bb05ba1645b1: its contact-surviving vector
p_test=(0,0,0,0,0,2,-1), with S=1, admits NO full first-order completion
Y. The obstruction is independent of the attempted W_test, any skew
parameterization or any support choice. It applies to all W and Y in
the original linearized cone.

The proof does not establish a neighborhood capacity bound. The exact
finite remainder still contains

    D2(p)+sym(L(Y)Tp),
    D2(p)=diag(-p_0^2,...,-p_6^2,-S^2).

A finite feasible perturbation may have zero first-order capacity change,
singular leading terms, or coefficient difference quotients not known
to be bounded. A separate argument must control these terms and all
nearby full certificates. The reconstructed equality condition above
is a concrete input for such a future argument, after verification.
The reconstruction also records affine elimination rank27; this is
preserved as finite algebraic telemetry, not promoted here to an
additional coercivity or neighborhood theorem.

No full strict positive-capacity tangent was found, so the sufficient
integration construction was not invoked and no finite seed parameter
was selected. A small numerical residual or an infeasibility label is
never used as a mathematical conclusion. The original target is still
OPEN, and the only deliverable eligible for a future formal gate is the
joint algebraic tangent theorem with all its explicit checks.

## 7. Persistence and literature process

The public directory is
/opt/danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/full_tangent_discovery_20261001/.
Its canonical ledger is the adjacent full_tangent_discovery_20261001_budget.json.
The raw manifest hashes every persisted source, model, raw solver object,
readback, exact certificate and log. Neither a hash nor a successful
program exit is a verifier acceptance. Shared-memory evidence will include
this entire manuscript and its exact appendix, rather than a private path.

The required Matlas search for PSD tangent/strict-complement perturbation
returned copositive-tensor and distributed-system statements with different
objects and hypotheses. Neither was used. The existing source's perturbation
background was already recalled. Official Clarabel documentation was read
for the solver encoding only. All mathematics used in this report is either
in the named accepted predecessor or is explicitly part of this joint
unverified derivation. There is no literature-based seed or local-optimality
claim. No new evaluation is permitted after the closed ledger.
